Marks finished tasks done (struck through) in the task ledger, refreshes the handoff cache, and appends to the log. Trigger with /wrapup.\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\nClose the current LSAT U / Aspiring Attorneys session. Leave the next session a clean, accurate handoff and mark completed work as done so it is never re-run. Resolve the vault root from the current workspace and its nearest instruction files; never assume a saved `~/ai-os/`, Windows, or Mac path. In this vault, read `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` and obey its index/log conventions.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Step 0: read the anchor\\r\\n\\r\\nBefore writing anything, re-read `wiki/tasks.md` and `wiki/hot.md` so the handoff reflects the real goal, not just the last thing touched. If a `plans/` file or README framed this session, skim it too.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Step 1: confirm task status changes only when needed\\r\\n\\r\\nFrom verified session work, determine whether task status changes are needed. If no task status changes are needed, leave `wiki/tasks.md` alone and continue with the authorized handoff and log updates without a confirmation step. If task status changes are proposed, show Joshua the concrete proposal before editing `wiki/tasks.md`:\\r\\n\\r\\n```\\r\\nProposing for wiki/tasks.md:\\r\\n  -> Done (strike through): <items you believe are finished this session>\\r\\n  -> Stays in Now: <still in progress>\\r\\n  -> New Next: <anything surfaced that should be queued>\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nWait for Joshua to approve or correct proposed task status changes, unless those exact changes were already approved in this session. Completion MUST be correct: a wrong \\"Done\\" is the exact failure this prevents. Do not mark something done on a guess; if unsure, leave its status unchanged and say so. This gate applies to task status changes, not to routine authorized handoff/log filing. If requesting approval, explain that this skill requires confirmation before changing task status.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Step 2: write the authorized handoff and log\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **`wiki/tasks.md`** - move approved finished items into `## Done`, wrapping each in `~~...~~` with a date, e.g. `~~Wire AI tutor into official drill surface~~ (2026-06-12)`. Update `## Now` and `## Next` only as approved. Preserve older task records; do not prune or delete them without explicit authorization. Skip this file when no task changes were approved or needed.\\r\\n2. **`wiki/hot.md`** - rewrite the `## Most recent (YYYY-MM-DD): <title>` block with 4 to 8 bullets of what changed. Refresh `## Active priorities` and `## Open threads` so they are true now.\\r\\n3. **`wiki/log.md`** - append a new entry at the BOTTOM of the log using `## [YYYY-MM-DD] system | Session wrapup`. Include source/output links, meaningful changed pages, verified results, unresolved work, and any skipped steps. Use Joshua\'s client date/time context. Preserve old entries exactly, including their legacy formats; never rewrite or reorder them. Multiple sessions per day are normal, so add a distinct title suffix when useful. If this session\'s authorized handoff and log entry already contain the same verified work, report them rather than duplicating the entry.\\r\\n4. **Task anchor** - if a README, plan, or wiki page advanced, make a surgical status update. Touch only what changed.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Step 3: confirm, then stop\\r\\n\\r\\n```\\r\\nHanded off: <1 line headline of this session>\\r\\nTasks: <N marked done, M still in Now>\\r\\nLogged: <the wiki/log.md entry label you added>\\r\\nNext session starts at: <the top open item in tasks.md / hot.md>\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- No em dashes. Terse. Surgical changes only (Karpathy: touch only what changed).\\r\\n- `raw/` is immutable; never write there.\\r\\n- `pickup` is the mirror skill for session start; run it only when explicitly requested.\\r\\n- Read current target contents before writing. Preserve unrelated changes. Run from the repository root with:\\r\\n    python system/scripts/check-student-records.py\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"\\r\\n\\r\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\r\\n\\r\\nimport re\\r\\nimport sys\\r\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\\r\\nSTUDENTS = ROOT / \\"students\\"\\r\\nWIKILINK = re.compile(r\\"\\\\[\\\\[([^\\\\]|]+)(?:\\\\|[^\\\\]]*)?\\\\]\\\\]\\")\\r\\nMARKDOWN_LINK = re.compile(r\\"(?<!!)\\\\[[^\\\\]]+\\\\]\\\\(([^)]+)\\\\)\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef section(markdown: str, heading: str) -> str | None:\\r\\n    match = re.search(rf\\"(?m)^## {re.escape(heading)}\\\\s*$\\", markdown)\\r\\n    if not match:\\r\\n        return None\\r\\n    end = re.search(r\\"(?m)^## \\", markdown[match.end() :])\\r\\n    return markdown[match.end() : match.end() + end.start()] if end else markdown[match.end() :]\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef linked_files(markdown: str, folder: Path) -> tuple[set[Path], list[str]]:\\r\\n    paths: set[Path] = set()\\r\\n    broken: list[str] = []\\r\\n    for target in WIKILINK.findall(markdown) + MARKDOWN_LINK.findall(markdown):\\r\\n        target = target.split(\\"#\\", 1)[0].strip()\\r\\n        if not target or target.startswith((\\"http://\\", \\"https://\\")):\\r\\n            continue\\r\\n        path = (ROOT / target) if target.startswith(\\"students/\\") else (folder / target)\\r\\n        if not path.name.endswith(\\".md\\"):\\r\\n            path = Path(f\\"{path}.md\\")\\r\\n        path = path.resolve()\\r\\n        paths.add(path)\\r\\n        if not path.is_file():\\r\\n            broken.append(target)\\r\\n    return paths, broken\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\ndef main() -> int:\\r\\n    errors: list[str] = []\\r\\n    folders = [\\r\\n        path\\r\\n        for path in sorted(STUDENTS.iterdir())\\r\\n        if path.is_dir() and not path.name.startswith(\\"_\\") and path.name != \\"non-students\\"\\r\\n    ]\\r\\n    exports = packages = 0\\r\\n\\r\\n    for folder in folders:\\r\\n        profile = folder / f\\"{folder.name}.md\\"\\r\\n        inventory = folder / \\"transcripts.md\\"\\r\\n        if not profile.is_file() or not inventory.is_file():\\r\\n            errors.append(f\\"{folder.name}: missing profile or transcripts.md\\")\\r\\n            continue\\r\\n\\r\\n        profile_section = section(profile.read_text(encoding=\\"utf-8\\"), \\"Open this record\\")\\r\\n        inventory_section = section(inventory.read_text(encoding=\\"utf-8\\"), \\"Local session files\\")\\r\\n        if profile_section is None:\\r\\n            errors.append(f\\"{folder.name}: profile lacks \'Open this record\'\\")\\r\\n            continue\\r\\n        if inventory_section is None:\\r\\n            errors.append(f\\"{folder.name}: transcripts.md lacks \'Local session files\'\\")\\r\\n            continue\\r\\n\\r\\n        profile_links, profile_broken = linked_files(profile_section, folder)\\r\\n        inventory_links, inventory_broken = linked_files(inventory_section, folder)\\r\\n        if inventory.resolve() not in profile_links:\\r\\n            errors.append(f\\"{folder.name}: profile does not link transcripts.md\\")\\r\\n        errors.extend(f\\"{folder.name}: broken profile link {link}\\" for link in profile_broken)\\r\\n        errors.extend(f\\"{folder.name}: broken session link {link}\\" for link in inventory_broken)\\r\\n\\r\\n        downloads = folder / \\"transcript-downloads\\"\\r\\n        for raw in sorted(downloads.rglob(\\"*.md\\")) if downloads.exists() else []:\\r\\n            exports += 1\\r\\n            if raw.resolve() not in inventory_links:\\r\\n                errors.append(f\\"{folder.name}: unlinked full export {raw.relative_to(folder)}\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n        sessions = folder / \\"sessions\\"\\r\\n        for package in sorted(sessions.iterdir()) if sessions.exists() else []:\\r\\n            if not package.is_dir():\\r\\n                continue\\r\\n            packages += 1\\r\\n            for name in (\\"transcript.md\\", \\"summary.md\\", \\"topic-map.md\\"):\\r\\n                file = package / name\\r\\n                if not file.is_file():\\r\\n                    errors.append(f\\"{folder.name}: incomplete package {package.name}/{name}\\")\\r\\n                elif file.resolve() not in inventory_links:\\r\\n                    errors.append(f\\"{folder.name}: unlinked package file {package.name}/{name}\\")\\r\\n\\r\\n    print(f\\"Checked {len(folders)} student folders, {exports} local Markdown exports, {packages} packages.\\")\\r\\n    if errors:\\r\\n        for error in errors:\\r\\n            print(f\\"ERROR {error}\\")\\r\\n        return 1\\r\\n    print(\\"All internal routers and local session links resolve.\\")\\r\\n"}'}]

She also found the Read, Recall, Colour instructions and report card unclear, so an attempted drill cannot yet be treated as a clean measure of the skill.\\r\\n- On the contract-negotiator question she reached A by chaining rules. Joshua distinguished a universal rule from a fact that triggers it: “every” and “whoever” establish implications, but they do not establish that a particular person satisfies the trigger.\\r\\n- In the incinerator question she hesitated between D and E. D followed from a stated claim about burning trash and wasting resources. E reversed the implication: absence of burning does not guarantee absence of resource waste (00:59:55–01:11:45).\\r\\n- Joshua promised additional conditional practice and revisions to the RC drill. Sofia planned further conditional work, RC practice, and an LR section; verify completion and any credited replacement sessions in live systems.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 2026-09-15 | Close the premise-to-conclusion bridge\\r\\n\\r\\nSource: [[transcript-downloads/2026-09-15-sofia-notes-by-gemini]].\\r\\n\\r\\n- Sofia struggled to recognize the gap in a freedom-of-thought Sufficient Assumption question. Joshua had her split the conclusion into its subject and judgment, trace the existing chain about intellectual progress and discipline, and identify the missing link to freedom of thought (00:01:25–00:28:55).\\r\\n- In the ball-lightning question, she had to see why some observed cases lacking the predicted properties could not support the universal claim that plasma is never a cause. The bridge needed to cover all instances, and a weak “can” claim would not do it (00:32:10–00:47:28).\\r\\n- In the social-spending flaw question, an alternative explanation did not refute the opponents\' independent reason. Sofia reported no parallel-flaw errors in her recent section after targeted review (00:51:06–00:59:52).\\r\\n- Sofia planned to reanalyze the freedom-of-thought and ball-lightning questions and book four meetings. Joshua said he would assign conditional practice and prepare RC material.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 2026-09-18 | Intermediate conclusions and sufficient assumptions\\r\\n\\r\\nSource: [[transcript-downloads/2026-09-18-sofia-notes-by-gemini]].\\r\\n\\r\\n- Sofia selected C on a political-systems assumption question and explained that at least one totalitarian system aiming to prevent conflict was needed for the counterexample. She reached it without a diagram (00:00:13–00:05:01).\\r\\n- In a tax-disclaimer question, she needed to test the branch where the email gave no illegal advice. Joshua showed that a sufficient assumption must close that uncovered branch too; A addressed it (00:09:27–00:22:11).\\r\\n- In the morality question, the missing link sat between a premise and an intermediate conclusion, not only at the final conclusion. In the art question, “symbolizes” was new in the conclusion and had to be connected to the relevance of intrinsic or extrinsic properties (00:27:41–01:01:52).\\r\\n- Sofia agreed to focus on hard Sufficient Assumption questions before a planned full practice test. Joshua promised a roughly ten-question mini boot camp. Verify assignment and test completion in LSAT U.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 2026-09-23 | Conditional chains and targeted section review\\r\\n\\r\\nSource: [[transcript-downloads/2026-09-23-sofia-notes-by-gemini]] (LSAT discussion through 00:50:16). The source continues with a separate conversation after Sofia leaves; that portion is not part of her teaching record.\\r\\n\\r\\n- Sofia reported stronger results on PT 126 sections than on harder recent sections and identified difficult Must Be True questions as a recurring problem. Joshua diagnosed conditional reasoning as the main source of errors and extra time, including on questions she answered correctly.\\r\\n- On a question about a literature professor and specialized training, Sofia drew arrows but made an unsupported connection between terms. She needs to test what each rule actually permits before chaining symbols.\\r\\n- For a Sufficient Assumption question with a conditional conclusion, Joshua had her separate its two sides, trace what the premises already establish, and find the bridge to the remaining side. Sofia recognized the correct answer when it expressed that bridge as a contrapositive.\\r\\n- She distinguished Sufficient Assumption from Must Be True: the former supplies a missing connection; the latter extracts a connection already supported by the given rules.\\r\\n- Joshua advised a section, immediate wrong-answer review, targeted repair, then another section. Sofia moved her planned full PrepTest from Friday to later in the week so she could focus on sections first.\\r\\n- Sofia said she had about 30 questions left in the conditional boot camp and planned to finish them, complete the Sufficient Assumption boot camp once assigned, and do a Logical Reasoning section. Verify completion in LSAT U before treating these as done.\\r\\n\\r\\n## 2026-09-25 | Proof direction and exact claims\\r\\n\\r\\nSource: [[transcript-downloads/2026-09-25-sofia-notes-by-gemini]].\\r\\n\\r\\n- Sofia initially associated “follows logically” with Sufficient Assumption. Joshua showed that in Must Be True the stimulus proves the answer, while in Sufficient Assumption the added answer premise proves the stated conclusion (00:05:43–00:08:39).
